Specifically, we model the road surface by placing 2D Gaussian surfels on a meshgrid, where each surfel explicitly stores color, semantic, and geometric information. Compared with conventional mesh-based representations and 3D Gaussian primitives, the proposed meshgrid Gaussian representation better matches the thin-surface property of roads while significantly reducing redundant primitives and overlap during optimization. To further improve representation efficiency and structural fidelity, we introduce a road-structure-aware adaptive meshgrid strategy, which allocates denser Gaussian surfels to geometrically or semantically complex regions, such as lane markings, road boundaries, and height discontinuities, while maintaining a compact representation in flat road areas. Moreover, instead of relying on a single nearest vehicle pose, we design a trajectory-consistency-guided pose-robust refinement strategy, which estimates local surface priors from multiple neighboring poses and adaptively weights pose-guided height regularization according to their geometric consistency.

</details>


### [73] [Beyond Single Expert: Harmonizing Diverse Visual Priors in MLLMs for Spatial Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15054)
*Xiao Lin,Xiaohu Huang,Kai Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ViPS提出了一种多模型先验框架，通过高效先验代理和动态先验融合机制，将多种视觉基础模型的互补空间先验知识融入多模态大语言模型，在空间推理和3D空间理解任务上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作通常仅整合单个预训练基础模型的先验知识来增强MLLM的空间理解能力。本文首先发现不同基础模型提供的空间先验具有互补性，能够服务于不同任务。因此，作者希望充分利用多种视觉模型的互补先验，以进一步提升MLLM的空间理解性能。

Method: ViPS框架包含两个核心模块：(1) 高效先验代理（Efficient Prior Proxy），以最小推理开销生成多种基础先验；(2) 动态先验融合机制（Dynamic Prior Fusion），实现和谐且上下文感知的先验融合与注入，使MLLM能够根据任务需求自适应地利用不同先验信息。

Result: 在多个复杂空间推理和3D空间理解基准上，ViPS成功调和了多样化的视觉先验，取得了新的最先进性能。

Conclusion: ViPS验证了融合多种互补视觉先验能够有效提升MLLM的空间理解能力，为多先验融合提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated substantial promise in spatial understanding. Existing works typically incorporate prior knowledge extracted from a pre-trained foundation model to further enhance the spatial awareness of MLLMs. In this paper, we first reveal that when integrating diverse foundation models into MLLMs, different models provide complementary spatial priors that benefit different tasks. Motivated by this, we propose $\textbf{ViPS}$, a novel multi-model prior framework designed to fully unleash the potential of incorporating multiple $\textbf{Vi}$sual $\textbf{P}$riors from diverse models into MLLMs for $\textbf{S}$patial understanding. Specifically, ViPS introduces an Efficient Prior Proxy to generate multiple foundational priors with minimal inference overhead, and a Dynamic Prior Fusion mechanism to achieve harmonious and context-aware prior fusion and injection from the prior proxies. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ViPS successfully harmonizes diverse visual priors, establishing new state-of-the-art performance across multiple complex spatial reasoning and 3D spatial understanding benchmarks. Project page: https://visual-ai.github.io/vips

</details>


### [74] [SUFLECA: Scaling Up Feature Learning for CAD-to-image Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15058)
*Saad Ejaz,Miguel Fernandez-Cortizas,Javier Civera,Holger Voos,Jose Luis Sanchez-Lopez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SUFLECA是一种弱监督的零样本CAD对齐框架，通过大规模几何特征学习（674K图像）和几何一致性匹配算法，在无需迭代位姿优化的前提下，实现了比现有零样本方法高10.3/12.2个百分点、甚至超越全监督方法的9D位姿估计性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本CAD对齐方法依赖外观驱动的特征匹配，在遮挡或仿真到真实域迁移场景下性能退化严重，缺乏几何引导的鲁棒特征表示。

Method: 1) 利用归一化物体坐标（NOCs）监督在674K图像（12个数据集）上从预训练视觉表示中扩展学习几何感知特征；2) 提出几何一致性匹配算法建立可靠的一对一CAD-图像对应关系。

Result: 在ScanNet25k上达到33.4%/42.3%的类别/实例精度，超越最强零样本基线10.3/12.2个百分点，且计算开销更小；首次在该基准上超越全监督方法。

Conclusion: 通过大规模几何特征学习和几何一致性匹配，SUFLECA证明了弱监督零样本方法在CAD对齐任务上可以达到甚至超越全监督方法的潜力，且无需迭代位姿优化。

Abstract: CAD-to-image alignment aims to estimate an object's 9D pose (rotation, translation, and anisotropic scale) from a single RGB image, enabling applications in robotics and augmented reality. Recent zero-shot methods use visual foundation models to match image regions to CAD models, yet typically their correspondences are appearance-driven and degrade under occlusion or sim-to-real domain shift. To address these limitations, we introduce SUFLECA (Scaling Up Feature LEarning for CAD Alignment), a weakly-supervised framework for zero-shot CAD alignment with two key contributions. First, SUFLECA scales up geometry-grounded feature learning from pretrained visual representations through Normalized Object Coordinates (NOCs) supervision on 674K images spanning 12 real and synthetic datasets, learning compact geometry-aware features that generalize across domains. Second, we propose a geometrically consistent matching algorithm that establishes reliable one-to-one CAD-to-image correspondences. Together, these contributions enable accurate, sub-second alignment per object instance without iterative pose refinement. On ScanNet25k, SUFLECA achieves 33.4%/42.3% category/instance accuracy, outperforming, with a smaller computational footprint, the strongest zero-shot baseline by 10.3/12.2 percentage points and, for the first time on this benchmark, even surpassing fully supervised methods. Code is available at: https://github.com/snt-arg/SUFLECA

</details>


### [75] [Towards Hierarchical Structure Understanding of Newspaper Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15082)
*William Mocaër,Solène Tarride,Thomas Constum,Merveilles Agbeti-Messan,Tom Simon,Clément Chatelain,Stéphane Nicolas,Pierrick Tranouez,Sébastien Cretin,Thierry Paquet*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种报纸结构理解方法：模块化流水线（YOLO+LayoutReader+自定义算法）和端到端Transformer架构Tiramisu，并发布了用于评估历史报纸层级信息检索的数据集Finlam La Liberté。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 报纸图像理解因复杂的嵌套层级结构和密集异构布局而具有挑战性，现有方法难以有效处理这种层次化信息。

Method: 1）模块化自底向上流水线：YOLO用于布局检测，LayoutReader用于阅读顺序预测，自定义算法用于文章分割。2）Tiramisu：基于分层Transformer的端到端架构，通过迭代层级过程显式建模文档层次结构，并行执行区域/文章分离、块定位、语义分类和阅读顺序预测。

Result: 两种方法均能有效重建复杂报纸层级结构，对比分析揭示了各自在可扩展文档数字化方面的优势。

Conclusion: 模块化方法具有灵活性和可解释性，Tiramisu通过并行注意力机制实现高效的端到端层次理解，两者互补适用于不同数字化场景。

Abstract: Understanding newspaper images remains a challenging task due to their complex, nested hierarchical structures and dense, heterogeneous layouts. In this paper, we explore two complementary approaches for newspaper structure understanding. First, we present a modular bottom-up pipeline that combines state-of-the-art open-source models: YOLO for layout detection, LayoutReader for reading order prediction, and a custom algorithm for article segmentation. This approach leverages existing robust components while maintaining flexibility and interpretability. Second, we introduce Tiramisu (Tiered Transformers for Hierarchical Structure Understanding), a novel end-to-end transformer-based architecture that explicitly models document hierarchy through an iterative tiered process. Tiramisu performs section and article separation, block localization, semantic categorization, and reading order prediction using highly parallelized attention mechanisms. Finally, we release Finlam La Liberté, a new dataset designed specifically for evaluating hierarchical information retrieval in historical newspapers. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of both approaches in reconstructing complex newspaper hierarchies, with comparative analysis highlighting their respective strengths for scalable document digitization. The Tiramisu training code, including the synthetic newspaper generator, is available at https://git.litislab.fr/tiramisu/tiramisu-newspaper-articles-extractor.

</details>


### [76] [Quantifying Training Membership Information in the Hyperspherical Embedding Geometry of Face Recognition Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15084)
*Ünsal Öztürk,Sébastien Marcel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了人脸识别模型中成员（训练集内身份）与非成员（训练集外身份）在单位超球面上嵌入向量的聚类几何特性差异。通过180个模型（涵盖不同IResNet骨干网络、损失函数、训练时长和训练身份数量）的因子设计实验，发现训练身份数量对成员/非成员可分性影响最大，而骨干网络和损失函数影响较小；在同一域内参考集上，几何成员信号随训练身份增加单调递减；跨域非成员基准会放大表观成员信号；融合四个几何统计量的学习分类器能揭示超越单一最优统计量的额外成员信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于角度间隔损失的人脸识别模型在训练中对成员身份形成紧密聚类，但非成员身份的聚类几何特性与成员存在差异，这种差异的量化及其受训练因素控制的程度尚不明确。

Method: 采用因子设计，在180个人脸识别模型上（改变IResNet骨干大小、损失函数头、训练时长和训练身份数量），计算四个基于聚类几何的统计量，并在9个基准上评估每个配置；最后用学习分类器融合四个统计量。

Result: 训练身份数量对成员/非成员可分性影响最大，骨干网络和损失函数头贡献很小；同域参考集上几何成员信号随训练身份增加单调递减；跨域非成员基准会放大表观成员信号；融合四个统计量的分类器优于任何单一统计量。

Conclusion: 训练身份数量是控制人脸识别模型中成员与非成员聚类几何差异的关键因素，跨域因素会引入偏差，融合多统计量可提升成员推断能力。

Abstract: Face recognition models represent each face as an embedding vector on the unit hypersphere by clustering embeddings of the same identity while pushing different identities apart through angular-margin losses. Because these losses act only on training identities, non-member identities may form clusters with different geometric properties. In this paper, we quantify the magnitude of this difference and what training-time factors control it. We compute four statistics based on cluster geometry across 180 face recognition models in a factorial design over IResNet backbone size, loss head, training duration, and the number of training identities, and evaluate each configuration on nine benchmarks. Our results indicate that the number of training identities has the largest effect on member/non-member separability, while backbone and loss head contribute far less, and that, on a same-domain held-out reference, the geometric membership signal decreases monotonically as more identities are added to training. We provide an analysis of cross-domain (pose, age, quality, ethnicity) non-member benchmarks and report that these inflate the apparent membership signal. Finally, we fuse all four statistics with a learned classifier to reveal additional membership information beyond the best individual statistic.

</details>


### [77] [AlphaWiSE: Adaptive Weight Interpolation for Continual Multimodal Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15094)
*Sarthak Jain,Qiran Hu,Zhen Zhu,Yaoyao Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AlphaWiSE，一种事后权重空间插值方法，通过拟合标量插值系数组合两个冻结的源检查点，实现多模态持续学习中的跨模态对齐保持，无需额外推理时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态模型（如CLIP）在持续适应新数据时，会破坏早期学到的跨模态对齐能力。现有持续学习方法返回单一检查点，迫使所有检索方向在稳定性-可塑性之间做出相同的权衡。

Method: AlphaWiSE是一种事后权重空间插值方法。它组合两个冻结的源检查点，对每个由检查点键标识的对齐参数张量，拟合一个由所有张量条目共享的标量插值系数。系数在小样本记忆上拟合，用于生成一个插值检查点。

Result: 在音频-图像-文本检索的大量实验中，AlphaWiSE在多个检索方向和评估指标上持续优于强持续学习基线。

Conclusion: AlphaWiSE能够在持续学习场景下有效保持跨模态对齐，且部署后的模型与任一源检查点具有相同的架构和参数量，不增加推理时间。

Abstract: Multimodal models such as CLIP learn a shared embedding space for cross-modal retrieval, but continual adaptation to sequentially arriving data can disrupt the cross-modal alignment acquired from earlier phases. Conventional continual-learning methods return a single checkpoint, which commits every retrieval direction to the same stability-plasticity trade-off. We propose AlphaWiSE, a post-hoc weight-space interpolation method that composes two frozen source checkpoints. For each aligned parameter tensor identified by its checkpoint key, AlphaWiSE fits one scalar interpolation coefficient shared by all tensor entries. The coefficients are fitted on a smaller exemplar memory and used to materialize one interpolated checkpoint. The deployed model has the same architecture and parameter count as either source checkpoint, which does not require additional inference time. Extensive experiments on audio-image-text retrieval show consistent improvements over strong continual-learning baselines across multiple retrieval directions and evaluation metrics.

</details>


### [78] [QuReC: All-in-One Image Restoration with Query-Specific Guidance and Local-Global Response Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15097)
*Shen Zhou,Jinghui Zhang,Wenbo Huang,Xuwei Qian,Zhen Wu,Guangwen Peng,Zhiyuan Li,Ding Ding,Dian Shen,Fang Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出QuReC统一框架，通过退化引导查询重建模块（DQRM）和局部-全局响应校准模块（LGRCM）实现全场景图像修复，在多种退化类型（包括空间异质和混合退化）上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全场景图像修复方法依赖图像级提示或共享引导，无法有效处理空间异质或混合形式的退化，且单纯的空间自适应引导不足以让查询可靠地聚合局部和全局的互补信息。

Method: 提出QuReC框架，包含(1)退化引导查询重建模块(DQRM)：将空间查询与退化原型空间匹配，重建查询特定的退化感知表示，并采用弱监督原型匹配学习策略优化稳定性；(2)局部-全局响应校准模块(LGRCM)：双分支聚合局部和全局特征，并用可学习先验校准响应，提升特征聚合可靠性与局部-全局协调能力。

Result: 在多个全场景图像修复基准上取得优于现有方法的性能。

Conclusion: QuReC通过细粒度空间自适应引导和局部-全局响应校准，有效解决了空间异质和混合退化下的全场景图像修复问题。

Abstract: All-in-one image restoration aims to recover clean images degraded by multiple corruption types using a single unified model. Existing methods typically rely on image-level prompts or shared guidance to handle diverse degradations. However, such a paradigm becomes inadequate when degradations are spatially heterogeneous or even coexist in mixed forms within a single image. Yet spatially adaptive guidance alone is not sufficient, since accurate restoration also requires each spatial query to reliably aggregate complementary information from local neighborhoods and global contexts. To this end, we propose QuReC, a unified framework for all-in-one image restoration. QuReC consists of a Degradation-Guided Query Reconstruction Module (DQRM) and a Local-Global Response Calibration Module (LGRCM). Specifically, DQRM matches each spatial query against a degradation prototype space to reconstruct a query-specific degradation-aware representation, thereby providing fine-grained spatially adaptive restoration guidance. To further stabilize this query-wise matching process, we introduce a weakly supervised prototype matching learning strategy to improve optimization stability and degradation semantic consistency. Meanwhile, LGRCM performs local-global dual-branch aggregation and calibrates the aggregated responses with learnable priors, improving the reliability of feature aggregation and the coordination between local detail modeling and global context modeling. Extensive experiments demonstrate that QuReC achieves superior performance on multiple all-in-one image restoration benchmarks. The code is released at https://github.com/zhoushen1/QuReC.

</details>


### [79] [DAPGNet: Dynamic Adaptive Physics-Guided Graph Diffusion Network for Hyperspectral Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15128)
*Pengkun Wang,Weijia Cao,Ning Wang,Xiaofei Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DAPGNet是一种动态自适应物理引导图扩散网络，通过将光谱物理先验注入图学习的高光谱图像分类方法，在四个基准数据集上显著优于CNN、Transformer、Mamba和图基基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图基高光谱分类器通常基于空间邻近性、超像素连通性或学习到的特征亲和力构建图拓扑，但忽略了连续波段携带的光谱物理先验对拓扑估计和消息传播的影响，导致分类精度受限。

Method: (1) 将连续光谱响应编码为节点级多尺度物理先验表示；(2) 两阶段图构造器结合光谱-空间亲和力、物理先验一致性和空间距离形成物理先验感知稀疏拓扑；(3) 图扩散中将边权重转化为加性注意力偏置，物理门控在节点级和特征级插值图聚合特征与投影物理先验特征；(4) 跨尺度融合集成不同扩散深度的节点状态；(5) 联合优化主分类损失、辅助监督和二阶光谱平滑正则化。

Result: 在Indian Pines、WHU-Hi-LongKou、Houston2013和Houston2018四个数据集上，OA、AA和Kappa指标均超越代表性CNN、Transformer、Mamba和图基基线方法，AA提升幅度达3.64~7.31个百分点。

Conclusion: 物理先验提取、先验感知拓扑构造、物理门控传播和光谱平滑正则化具有互补效应，共同提升了高光谱图像分类性能。

Abstract: Hyperspectral image (HSI) classification requires reliable pixel-relation modeling under spectral variability, mixed pixels, and heterogeneous boundaries. Existing graph-based HSI classifiers usually construct graph topology from spatial proximity, superpixel connectivity, or learned feature affinity. However, the spectral physical prior carried by contiguous bands has limited influence on topology estimation and message propagation. This paper presents DAPGNet, a dynamic adaptive physics-guided graph diffusion network that injects a structure-constrained physical prior into relation-level graph learning. DAPGNet first encodes contiguous spectral responses into node-wise multiscale physical-prior representations. A two-stage graph constructor then combines spectral-spatial affinity, physical-prior consistency, and spatial distance to form a physical-prior-aware sparse topology. During graph diffusion, learned edge weights are transformed into additive attention biases, while a physical gate performs node-wise and feature-wise interpolation between graph-aggregated features and projected physical-prior features. Cross-scale fusion integrates node states from different diffusion depths, and the network is optimized with main classification, auxiliary supervision, and second-order spectral smoothness regularization. Experiments on Indian Pines, WHU-Hi-LongKou, Houston2013, and Houston2018 show that DAPGNet achieves the best OA, AA, and Kappa among representative CNN-, Transformer-, Mamba-, and graph-based baselines. It improves AA over the strongest competing method by 3.64 to 7.31 percentage points across the four datasets. Ablation and sensitivity analyses further support the complementary effects of physical-prior extraction, prior-aware topology construction, physics-gated propagation, and spectral smoothness regularization.

</details>


### [80] [Ray-based phase error correction for miniaturized DOE projector-based FPP under single-directional hyperbolic projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15139)
*Seung-Jae Son,Yatong An,Jae-Sang Hyun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对使用微型DOE投影仪的条纹投影轮廓测量系统，提出了一种基于射线的相位误差校正框架，通过投影仪针孔估计和单姿态校准，显著提升了非线性投影条件下的重建精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微型DOE投影仪的非线性投影特性和有限的图案可控性会导致严重的相位伪影，传统图像域处理方法难以有效校正。

Method: 提出射线级相位误差校正框架，沿投影射线建模相位伪影；设计基于单向双曲线条纹图案的投影仪针孔估计方法，无需立体标定即可恢复投影仪几何；采用数据高效策略，仅需单次校准姿态即可构建精化模型。

Result: 在微型DOE投影仪FPP系统上的实验表明，该方法在非线性投影条件下显著提升了重建精度，验证了方法的鲁棒性和物理一致性。

Conclusion: 所提出的射线级校正框架能有效解决微型DOE投影仪带来的相位伪影问题，是一种物理一致且数据高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Fringe Projection Profilometry (FPP) systems using miniaturized DOE pro-jectors often suffer from severe phase artifacts due to nonlinear projection characteristics and limited pattern controllability. We propose a ray-based phase error correction framework that models phase artifacts along projection rays from the projector pinhole, incorporating projector geometry without re-lying on image-domain processing or neighboring pixels. A projector pinhole estimation method based on a single-directional hyperbolic fringe pattern is introduced, through which projector geometry can be recovered without stereo calibration. In addition, a data-efficient strategy constructs the re-finement model from a single calibration pose. Experiments on miniaturized DOE projector-based FPP systems demonstrate significant improvements in reconstruction accuracy under nonlinear projection conditions, confirming the robustness and physical consistency of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [81] [MAGiSt3R: Multi-Agent Feed-forward 3D Reconstruction from Monocular RGB Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15211)
*Ziren Gong,Xiaohan Li,Fabio Tosi,Ninghui Xu,Stefano Mattoccia,Jianfei Cai,Matteo Poggi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MAGiSt3R是一个多智能体3D重建框架，利用3R系列的前馈模型和MAGMA合并模型，以接近10 FPS的速度从单目RGB视频中同时进行重建和相机跟踪，并通过位姿图优化减少累积漂移，在合成和真实数据集上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现单目RGB视频的快速、高精度3D重建与相机跟踪，解决现有前馈流水线中累积相机漂移的问题。

Method: 采用多智能体架构，使用3R系列前馈模型从RGB视频回归局部点图，通过MAGMA模型在智能体内部和智能体间合并局部点图得到全局点图，并利用位姿图优化减轻累积相机漂移。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上，MAGiSt3R在重建精度和相机跟踪精度上均优于当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: MAGiSt3R通过多智能体框架和位姿图优化，有效提升了单目RGB视频3D重建的速度和准确性。

Abstract: This paper presents MAGiSt3R, a multi-agent 3D reconstruction framework performing reconstruction and camera tracking for monocular RGB videos at almost 10 FPS. MAGiSt3R relies on a feed-forward model from the 3R family to process RGB videos and regress local point maps, and on a merging model, MAGMA, that combines local maps at both intra-agent and inter-agent levels to obtain the final global point map. Furthermore, MAGiSt3R performs pose graph optimization to mitigate cumulative camera drift occurring along the feed-forward pipeline. We evaluate MAGiSt3R on both synthetic and real-world datasets, demonstrating its superior reconstruction and camera tracking accuracy compared to state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [82] [Symbal: Detecting Systematic Misalignments in Model-Generated Captions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15216)
*Maya Varma,Jean-Benoit Delbrouck,Sophie Ostmeier,Akshay Chaudhari,Curtis Langlotz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出系统性错位检测任务，用于识别多模态大模型在图像描述中反复出现的错误模式。作者构建了Symbal方法（利用现成基础模型进行双阶段检测）和SymbalBench基准（含170万图像-文本对、420个数据集），Symbal在基准上达到63.8%的正确识别率，比最强基线提升近4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型生成的图像描述常出现错误，导致图文对齐不准确。现有工作缺乏对一类称为"系统性错位"（即特定视觉特征与特定描述错误之间存在强关联的重复性错误）的检测方法，因此本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 提出Symbal方法，采用结构化双阶段流程，利用现成的预训练基础模型来识别系统性错位，并以自然语言总结结果。同时构建SymbalBench基准，包含两个领域（自然图像和医学图像）的170万图像-文本对，组织成420个具有标注系统性错位的数据集。

Result: Symbal在SymbalBench上正确识别63.8%数据集中的系统性错位，比最强基线提升近4倍。在真实场景评估中，Symbal能准确识别四个不同多模态大模型生成的描述中的系统性错位，并有效用于审计现成的图像-描述数据集。

Conclusion: 本文提出的新任务、方法和基准为审计多模态大模型生成的图像描述提供了有效工具，能在不访问底层模型的情况下帮助用户识别关键错误，代码已开源。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) often introduce errors when generating image captions, resulting in misaligned image-text pairs. Our work focuses on a class of captioning errors that we refer to as systematic misalignments, where a recurring error in MLLM-generated captions is closely associated with the presence of a specific visual feature in the paired image. Given a vision-language dataset with MLLM-generated captions, our aim in this work is to detect such errors, a task we refer to as systematic misalignment detection. As our first key contribution, we present Symbal, which utilizes a structured, dual-stage setup with off-the-shelf foundation models to identify systematic misalignments and summarize results in natural language. As our second key contribution, we introduce SymbalBench, a benchmark designed to evaluate automated methods on our proposed task. SymbalBench consists of 1.7 million image-text pairs from two domains (natural and medical images), organized into 420 vision-language datasets with annotated systematic misalignments. Symbal exhibits strong performance on this benchmark, correctly identifying systematic misalignments in 63.8% of datasets, a nearly 4x improvement over the closest baseline. We supplement our evaluations on SymbalBench with real-world evaluations, showing that (1) Symbal can accurately surface systematic misalignments in captions generated by four MLLMs and (2) Symbal is a powerful tool for auditing off-the-shelf image-caption datasets. Ultimately, our novel task, method, and benchmark can aid users with auditing MLLM-generated captions and identifying critical errors, without requiring access to the underlying MLLM. Code is available at https://github.com/Stanford-AIMI/Symbal.

</details>


### [83] [Structural-Semantic Reciprocal Learning for Unsupervised Visible-Infrared Person Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15220)
*Moyao Tian,Shijia Liu,Yan Yang,Xin Yuan,Minshi Chen,Wei Wang,Xiao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出结构-语义互学习(SSRL)框架，通过细粒度结构解耦(FSD)提取身体部位基元作为空间锚点，并设计闭环语义校准(CSC)机制过滤伪标签噪声，将开放环路关联转变为自校正闭环系统，在USVI-ReID任务上达到SOTA水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督可见光-红外行人重识别方法存在两个瓶颈：依赖模糊的全局表征，以及伪标签噪声在开环方式下不受抑制地传播，导致跨模态关联不准确。

Method: 提出SSRL框架，包含两个核心模块：1) 细粒度结构解耦(FSD)，提取判别性身体部位基元作为可靠的空间锚点，补充全局轮廓的细节信息；2) 闭环语义校准(CSC)，在每个epoch重建共享语义原型并反馈回训练循环，在下一次聚类前过滤伪标签噪声。通过结构学习与语义学习的互反交互实现鲁棒的跨模态表示。

Result: 在SYSU-MM01和RegDB数据集上超越现有SOTA方法，且在RegDB上超过若干有监督方法。

Conclusion: 结构-语义互学习机制有效解决了开环关联中的噪声累积问题，闭环校准与细粒度结构信息互补，为实现鲁棒的跨模态无监督行人重识别提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Unsupervised visible-infrared person re-identification (USVI-ReID) is challenging due to the large modality gap and the lack of cross-modal identity annotations. Progressive association paradigms have been proposed to gradually bridge the gap, but they suffer from two critical bottlenecks: reliance on ambiguous global representations and unchecked propagation of pseudo-label noise in an open-loop manner. To address these issues, we propose Structural-Semantic Reciprocal Learning (SSRL), a framework that transforms open-loop association into a self-correcting closed-loop system. Structurally, we introduce Fine-grained Structural Decoupling (FSD) to extract discriminative body-part primitives as reliable spatial anchors, complementing ambiguous holistic silhouettes with spatially consistent structural details. Semantically, we design a Closed-loop Semantic Calibration (CSC) mechanism that reconstructs shared semantic prototypes at each epoch and feeds them back into the training loop, effectively filtering pseudo-label noise before the next clustering cycle. Through the reciprocal interaction between structural and semantic learning, SSRL achieves robust cross-modal representation. Extensive experiments demonstrate the competitive performance of SSRL against state-of-the-art USVI-ReID methods on both SYSU-MM01 and RegDB, notably surpassing several supervised counterparts on RegDB.

</details>


### [84] [Divergent Gaze Patterns in Artistic Viewing: Spatial and Temporal Signatures of Attention Across Autistic Individuals, Artists, and Neurotypical Observers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15227)
*Mohammed Amine Kerkouri,Daphné Senggaran,Renaud Jusiak,Océane Lehmann,Marouane Tliba,Claire Wardak,Emmanuelle Houy-Durand,Shasha Morel-Kohlmeyer,Aladine Chetouani,Nadia Aguillon-Hernandez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究比较了自闭症成人、训练有素的艺术家和神经典型观众在自由观看30幅画作时的眼动模式，从空间（注视密度图）和时间（扫描路径）两个维度进行分析。结果发现艺术家与神经典型观众几乎无法区分，而自闭症组表现出独特的注意力特征：空间探索范围与艺术家相似，但时间模式不同（注视时间更短、更不稳定、个体间一致性最低）。自闭症注视模式并非类似艺术家，而是更接近神经典型观众，表明自闭症对艺术品的观看是一种独特的群体特异性注意特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有自闭症眼动研究多使用社交场景而非艺术品，且主要分析注视位置而忽略时间顺序。本文旨在系统比较自闭症成人、艺术家和神经典型观众在自由观看绘画时的空间和时间眼动模式，以揭示自闭症艺术审美注意的独特性。

Method: 招募三组被试（自闭症成人、训练有素的艺术家、神经典型对照），每组观看30幅画作15秒。空间维度：用六种显著性指标（AUC-Judd, NSS, CC, SIM, KL, 信息增益）比较各组注视密度图的预测能力；时间维度：用MultiMatch、ScanMatch、FDISS和DTW比较个体扫描路径。所有组均采用统一的离散阈值算法提取注视点。

Result: (i)艺术家与神经典型组在空间（密度图相关CC=0.96）和时间（扫描路径对齐性最高）上几乎不可区分，而ASD组与两者均不同；(ii)ASD注意力呈现分离模式：空间探索范围与艺术家相似（分散度、探索面积），但时间特征不同（注视更短、停留更少、扫描路径个体间一致性最低）；(iii)ASD注视并非在任一指标上选择性类似艺术家，反而更接近神经典型组。

Conclusion: 自闭症成人对艺术品的观看构成了一种独特的群体特异性注意特征，在空间和时间维度上均有别于艺术家和神经典型观众。研究结果支持对审美注意进行群体条件化建模，并提供了公开的分析代码和刺激级结果。

Abstract: How different populations visually explore artworks bears on cognitive science and on accessibility design, yet most eye-tracking work in autism has used social scenes rather than art, and has analysed where the eyes land while ignoring when and in what order. We present a comparative free-viewing study across three groups, autistic adults (ASD), trained artists, and neurotypical observers, who each viewed 30 paintings for 15s. We introduce a directed, metric-grounded framework that compares groups along two complementary axes: a spatial axis, in which one group's fixation-density map predicts another's fixations under six saliency metrics (AUC-Judd, NSS, CC, SIM, KL, Information Gain); and a temporal axis, in which individual scanpaths are compared with MultiMatch, ScanMatch, a foveal-disc IoU score (FDISS), and dynamic time warping (DTW). Fixations are extracted uniformly for all groups with a dispersion-threshold algorithm. Three results converge. (i)Artists and neurotypicals are almost indistinguishable in both space (density-map correlation CC=0.96) and time (they form the most alignable scanpath pair), whereas ASD gaze diverges from both. (ii)ASD attention is dissociated: it matches artists' wide spatial exploration (dispersion, explored area) but carries a distinct temporal signature, shorter fixations, less dwell, and the most idiosyncratic (least self-consistent) scanpaths of any group. (iii)ASD gaze is not selectively artist-like on any metric; if anything it is marginally closer to neurotypical. Together these findings indicate that autistic viewing of art is a distinct, group-specific attentional profile in both space and time, and they motivate population-conditioned models of aesthetic attention. We release all analysis code and per-stimulus results.

</details>


### [85] [CRISP: Constrained Refinement via Iterative Squeezing Process for Robust Medical Image Segmentation under Domain Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15231)
*Yizhou Fang,Pujin Cheng,Yixiang Liu,Xiaoying Tang,Longxi Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CRISP框架，基于“正区域排序稳定性”假设，通过概率排名稳定性推导鲁棒空间先验，在无需目标域数据和测试时参数更新的情况下实现源域仅分割，显著提升多中心、人口统计和模态分布偏移下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学影像中的分布偏移导致临床AI性能严重下降且加剧健康不平等，现有域适应方法受限于预定义的模拟偏移或伪监督，无法应对真实世界中无限且不可预测的分布偏移。

Method: 基于“正区域排序稳定性”假设，CRISP通过潜在特征扰动提取扰动不变的高精度（HP）核心区域和至少一次扰动下仍为前景的高召回（HR）支持区域，利用这两种先验在扰动下递归细化，并设计迭代训练框架逐步收缩HP和HR至最终分割。

Result: 在多中心心脏MRI和CT肺血管分割任务上，CRISP显著优于SOTA方法，在多中心、人口统计和模态偏移下HD95分别降低0.14（7.0%）、1.90（13.1%）和8.39（38.9%）像素。

Conclusion: CRISP作为模型无关的即插即用框架，无需测试时参数更新和目标域数据，通过利用概率排名的稳定性有效解决了开放世界中的分布偏移问题，为医学AI的临床转化提供了新思路。

Abstract: Distribution shift in medical imaging remains a central bottleneck for the clinical translation of medical AI. Failure to address it can lead to severe performance degradation in unseen environments and exacerbate health inequities. Existing methods for domain adaptation are inherently limited by exhausting predefined possibilities through simulated shifts or pseudo-supervision. Such strategies struggle in the open-ended and unpredictable real world, where distribution shifts are effectively infinite. To address this challenge, we adopt the "Rank Stability of Positive Regions" as a working assumption under distribution shift, and use it to derive robust spatial hints for source-only segmentation. Guided by this assumption, we propose CRISP, a model-agnostic framework that, unlike deployment-time adaptation, requires no test-time parameter updates and no target-domain data--a target-free, plug-in refinement framework that segments with frozen weights. Rather than using ranking to directly output masks, CRISP exploits the stability of probability rankings under distribution shift to derive robust spatial priors. Via latent feature perturbation, perturbation-invariant high-grade regions define a high-precision (HP) core, while voxels that remain potentially foreground under at least one perturbation define a high-recall (HR) support; these dual priors are then recursively refined under perturbation. We then design an iterative training framework that progressively squeezes HP and HR toward the final segmentation. Extensive evaluations on multi-center cardiac MRI and CT-based lung vessel segmentation demonstrate CRISP's superior robustness, significantly outperforming state-of-the-art methods with striking HD95 reductions of up to 0.14 (7.0% improvement), 1.90 (13.1% improvement), and 8.39 (38.9% improvement) pixels across multi-center, demographic, and modality shifts, respectively.

</details>


### [86] [ARMOR++: Agentic Orchestration of a Multi-Domain Primitive Set for Transferable Attacks on Deepfake Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15246)
*Christos Korgialas,Gabriel Lee Jun Rong,Dion Jia Xu Ho,Pai Chet Ng,Xiaoxiao Miao,Konstantinos N. Plataniotis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ARMOR++框架，利用Qwen2.5-VL视觉语言模型和Qwen3大语言模型协同驱动多原语攻击，在无查询限制的黑盒条件下显著提升深度伪造检测器对抗迁移的攻击成功率，揭示了当前检测器部署中存在的可靠性缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑盒对抗迁移攻击针对深度伪造检测器的可靠性差，攻击方法缺乏语义感知能力，在无查询限制下从CNN代理模型向Transformer目标模型迁移时效果不佳。

Method: ARMOR++采用多智能体框架：Qwen2.5-VL提供空间语义先验，Qwen3负责原语选择、自适应超参数重参数化和熵正则化扰动混合，集成五种互补攻击原语（密集优化、显著性方法、空间变换、频域扰动、块结构修改）。

Result: 在AADD-2025基准上，低质量与高质量图像场景下，ARMOR++在盲目标攻击成功率上显著超越现有基于智能体和非智能体的基线方法，并在鲁棒防御配置下仍保持性能优势。

Conclusion: 当前深度伪造检测器部署存在显著的可靠性缺陷，智能体协同编排能够有效识别潜在脆弱性，是一种有效的攻击范式。

Abstract: The reliability of deepfake detectors frequently degrades under black-box adversarial transfer, as these models often rely on fragile, architecture-dependent forensic cues. Existing transfer attacks often lack semantic awareness and struggle to maintain effectiveness under strict no-query constraints, particularly when perturbations are transferred from convolutional surrogates to transformer-based targets. To address these limitations, this paper introduces ARMOR++, a robust multi-agent framework designed for high-transferability deepfake evasion. The framework leverages the Qwen2.5-VL Vision-Language Model (VLM) to supply spatial semantic priors, while the Qwen3 Large Language Model (LLM) orchestrates primitive selection, adaptive hyperparameter reparameterization, and entropy-regularized perturbation mixing. By integrating five complementary primitives, spanning dense optimization, saliency-based methods, spatial transformations, frequency-domain perturbations, and block-structured modifications, ARMOR++ effectively targets heterogeneous inductive biases. Rigorous evaluation on the AADD-2025 benchmark demonstrates that ARMOR++ significantly outperforms existing agentic and non-agentic baselines across both low- and high-quality image regimes. Statistical analysis confirms a substantial gain in blind-target Attack Success Rate (ASR) over the state-of-the-art agentic baseline, with further performance advantages evidenced against non-agentic benchmarks and under robust defensive configurations. These findings highlight a significant residual reliability gap in current deepfake detector deployments and demonstrate the efficacy of agentic orchestration in identifying latent vulnerabilities.

</details>


### [87] [HoloGeo: Mitigating Landmark Bias in Geo-localization via Evidence-Driven Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15255)
*Pengcheng Zhou,Xuanyu Liu,Yanchen Yin,Bobo Li,Shengqiong Wu,Mong-Li Lee,Wynne Hsu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对视觉语言模型在地理定位中存在的标志性建筑偏差问题，设计了偏差强度（BI）和偏差危害性（BH）两个量化指标，构建了LandmarkBias-3K基准数据集，并提出基于证据驱动的推理框架HoloGeo，辅以BF-30k高质量数据集和多维奖励机制，实现了对多样化视觉线索的均衡关注和联合推理，在多个基准上取得优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在地理定位任务中容易受到标志性建筑偏差的影响，导致忽略地理线索或形成虚假关联，造成定位不准确。目前缺乏对这一问题的系统性量化和有效的缓解方法。

Method: 首先设计偏差强度（BI）和偏差危害性（BH）两个量化指标，构建LandmarkBias-3K基准数据集；提出HoloGeo证据驱动推理框架，基于BF-30k高质量数据集（带有结构化多证据无偏差推理链注释），通过多维奖励机制鼓励对多样化视觉线索的均衡关注，实现证据驱动的联合推理。

Result: HoloGeo在IM2GPS3K和YFCC4k上保持优异性能，在LandmarkBias-3K上显著优于现有开源视觉语言模型，验证了其在稳健地理空间推理中的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的量化指标和HoloGeo框架能有效缓解标志性建筑偏差，提升地理定位的鲁棒性和可靠性。

Abstract: Recent advances in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have significantly improved image geo-localization, yet existing models remain susceptible to landmark bias, causing them to overlook geographical cues or form spurious correlations, ultimately resulting in inaccurate localization. To systematically investigate this issue, we first design two quantitative metrics, Bias Intensity (BI) and Bias Harmfulness (BH), to characterize the impact of landmarks exerted on model reasoning, and establish a comprehensive benchmark, LandmarkBias-3K. To mitigate landmark bias, we further propose an evidence-driven reasoning framework, HoloGeo, to improve the reliability of geo-localization. HoloGeo is supported by a high-quality dataset, BF-30k, annotated with structured multi-evidence bias-free reasoning chains. By incorporating multi-dimensional rewards, HoloGeo explicitly encourages balanced attention over diverse visual cues and achieves evidence-driven joint reasoning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that HoloGeo not only maintains excellent performance on IM2GPS3K and YFCC4k but also significantly outperforms existing open-source VLMs on LandmarkBias-3K, validating its effectiveness for robust geospatial reasoning.

</details>


### [88] [SceneBind: Binding What and Where Across Vision, Audio and Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15265)
*Mingfei Chen,Zijun Cui,Ruoke Zhang,Hyeonggon Ryu,Eli Shlizerman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SceneBind提出了一种全模态场景表示方法，通过结合全局语义嵌入与以对象为中心的语义-空间槽位，实现了跨视觉、音频和语言模态的联合语义与3D空间理解，在场景检索和空间定位任务上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全模态编码器擅长实例级语义理解（"有什么"），但缺乏显式的空间结构信息（"在哪里"）。SceneBind旨在弥合这一差距，构建兼具语义与空间理解的场景表示。

Method: 将每个场景表示为语义-空间实体，包含全局语义嵌入和以对象为中心的语义-空间槽位，显式捕获对象级语义、空间属性及不确定性。提出SceneBind Matching方案，结合全局场景相似度与对象对齐，支持跨模态场景检索和对象定位。构建了真实世界双耳音视频数据集，并提出跨模态语义与空间信号对齐的训练协议。

Result: 在场景检索和空间检索任务上达到最优性能，支持下游任务（如音视频定位）的强零样本迁移能力。与大规模预训练语义编码器兼容，仅需少量额外token即可实现轻量级空间建模。

Conclusion: SceneBind通过语义-空间联合表示有效填补了全模态场景理解中空间结构的缺失，证明了在跨模态场景检索和空间定位方面的有效性。

Abstract: We present SceneBind, an omni-modal representation of realistic scenes with joint semantic and 3D spatial understanding across vision, audio and language. Existing omni-modal encoders excel at instance-level semantics (i.e., what is present), but often lack explicit spatial structure (i.e., where it is). SceneBind addresses this gap by representing each scene as a semantic-spatial entity, combining a global semantic embedding with object-centric semantic-spatial slots. This representation explicitly captures object-level semantics, spatial attributes, and uncertainty. We further propose SceneBind Matching, a semantic-spatial matching scheme that integrates global scene similarity with object alignment, supporting cross-modal scene retrieval and object grounding. To train and evaluate SceneBind, we curate a novel real-world binaural audio-visual dataset with structured semantic and spatial annotations, and propose a training protocol for aligning semantic and spatial signals across modalities. SceneBind is compatible with large-scale pretrained semantic encoders, adds lightweight spatial modeling with only a few additional tokens. It achieves state-of-the-art scene and spatial retrieval while enabling strong zero-shot transfer to downstream tasks such as audio-visual localization.

</details>


### [89] [Motion-Conditioned Multi-View Fusion for Myocardial Infarction Localization from Echocardiography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15268)
*Guang Yang,Wentian Xu,Siyu Wang,Betty Raman,Lei Li,Vicente Grau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MCF-Net 提出了一种运动引导的多视图融合框架，利用极稀疏监督的心肌运动建模和预训练超声心动图基础模型，在节段水平心肌梗死定位中实现了72.4% F1和84.9%准确率，优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖手工描述符或密集监督的运动估计，标注需求大；基础模型虽提升了视觉分析，但单视图和节段级定位在视图依赖模糊性下不可靠，尤其在心尖切面。

Method: 使用EchoPrime双视图共享提取视觉特征；以单张标注模板帧跨视频传递初始化点跟踪实现极稀疏运动监督；运动导出的节段感知软掩码提供粗粒度空间先验增强困难节段特征；运动条件融合机制跨视图集成运动与视觉信息。

Result: 节段水平MI定位F1 72.4%，准确率84.9%，超越了运动-only、视觉-only及融合基线方法。

Conclusion: MCF-Net通过运动引导的多视图融合有效解决了视图依赖模糊性，在极稀疏监督下实现了精准的节段级MI定位。

Abstract: Myocardial infarction (MI) remains a leading cause of mortality worldwide. Echocardiography (Echo) is a widely available modality for MI assessment, where regional wall motion abnormality is a key indicator. Prior learning based methods for myocardial motion analysis often use handcrafted descriptors or densely supervised estimation, but the need for extensive annotation limits applicability. Foundation models have recently improved vision-based Echo analysis; however, most methods operate on single views and segment-level localization remains unreliable under view-dependent ambiguity, especially in apical views. To address this, we propose MCF-Net, a novel motion-guided multi-view fusion framework that fuses myocardial motion cues with foundation model representations to localize infarction. Visual features are extracted using EchoPrime, a pretrained Echo foundation model shared across dual views. Cardiac motion is modeled with extremely sparse supervision: a single annotated template frame is transferred across videos to initialize point tracking, avoiding dense labels. Motion-derived segment-aware soft masks provide coarse spatial priors that selectively enhance features for challenging myocardial segments. A motion-conditioned fusion mechanism then integrates motion and vision across views, refining predictions without overriding strong appearance cues. On segment-level MI localization, MCF-Net achieves 72.4\% F1 and 84.9\% accuracy, outperforming state-of-the-art motion-only, vision-only, and fusion baselines.

</details>


### [90] [Online Neural Space Time Memory for Dynamic Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15271)
*Baback Elmieh,Lynn Tsai,Zeman Li,Srinivas Kaza,Tiancheng Sun,Gabor Csapo,Ali Behrouz,Yuan Deng,Stephen Lombardi,Steven M. Seitz,Xuan Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于多视角流式视频的实时新视图合成方法，通过将记忆更新与记忆应用解耦，采用周期性的记忆更新和逐帧记忆应用，结合跨视角注意力、辅助记忆损失和记忆缓存策略，在动态场景中实现了实时、最先进的性能，并支持分钟级的在线记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线新视图合成面临长期记忆与实时性之间的根本权衡：需要维持持久记忆以重建被遮挡区域，同时需满足严格的实时约束。标准TTT模型每帧进行梯度更新，计算成本过高，且长上下文中存在不稳定性。

Method: 1) 将记忆更新（高频密集型）与记忆应用（逐帧执行）解耦，记忆采用周期性更新而非每帧更新。2) 使用跨视角注意力处理当前帧与先前记忆状态之间的形变。3) 引入辅助记忆损失（Memory Loss）迫使模型持久内化场景。4) 采用记忆缓存策略（Memory Caching）正则化主动权重，防止灾难性漂移。

Result: 在动态人体运动场景中实现了实时的、最先进的性能，并支持分钟级在线记忆。

Conclusion: 通过解耦记忆更新与应用频率，结合辅助损失和缓存正则化，有效平衡了长期记忆保持与实时计算约束，为在线新视图合成提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Online novel view synthesis from multi-view streaming videos faces a fundamental trade-off: maintaining a persistent, long-horizon memory to reconstruct temporarily occluded regions while operating under strict real-time constraints. While Test-Time Training (TTT) offers a powerful memory mechanism, standard models mandate gradient-based memory updates at every frame to adapt to the changing motion in dynamic scenes. The computational cost of heavy memory updates precludes real-time application and can lead to instability over long contexts. Given that memory updates are more demanding than memory application and video content is largely redundant, we propose to decouple the frequencies of these two processes. Our approach performs periodic memory updates while applying the memory on a per-frame basis, using cross-view attention to manage deformations between the prior memory state and the current frame. To lock in the historical context, we introduce two critical mechanisms: an auxiliary Memory Loss that forces persistent internalization of the scene, and a Memory Caching strategy that regularizes active weights against catastrophic drift. Our method demonstrates real-time, state-of-the-art performance on scenes with dynamic human motion as well as minute-scale online memorization.

</details>


### [91] [MeanFlowNFT: Bringing Forward-Process RL to Average-Velocity Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15273)
*Yushi Huang,Xiangxin Zhou,Jun Zhang,Liefeng Bo,Tianyu Pang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MeanFlowNFT，通过构建诱导瞬时速度预测器，将DiffusionNFT的强化学习对齐框架应用于平均速度采样的MeanFlow生成器，在保持快速少步生成的同时实现奖励优化，并继承了严格策略改进保证，在图像和视频生成任务上超越基线及现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MeanFlow生成器通过预测时间区间内的平均速度实现快速少步采样，但现有强化学习对齐方法（如DiffusionNFT）优化的是瞬时速度，无法直接应用于MeanFlow框架。为弥补这一差距，需要一种能将RL奖励优化与MeanFlow的平均速度采样相结合的方法。

Method: 受MeanFlow恒等式（桥接平均速度与瞬时速度）启发，构建诱导瞬时速度预测器，将DiffusionNFT目标函数应用于该预测器，使得奖励优化对MeanFlow有良好定义；采样仍基于平均速度，保持MeanFlow的快速少步生成能力。

Result: 在图像和视频生成任务上，MeanFlowNFT一致性地改进了基线方法；在SD3.5-M上，8项指标中6项超越现有最先进的RL微调少步生成器；在Wan 2.1上，4步MeanFlowNFT达到84.33的VBench分数，超过了50步LongCat-Video RL的82.57。

Conclusion: MeanFlowNFT成功将RL对齐扩展到MeanFlow框架，继承了DiffusionNFT的严格策略改进保证，并在少步生成场景下达到了甚至超越多步RL微调扩散模型的性能，展示了高效生成与奖励优化的有效结合。

Abstract: MeanFlow generators achieve fast few-step sampling by predicting average velocities over time intervals, making them attractive for efficient generation. Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a powerful way to align diffusion and flow models with human preferences and task-specific objectives. In particular, DiffusionNFT offers an efficient forward-process RL framework that does not require reverse-process trajectories or likelihood estimation. However, applying such RL methods to MeanFlow remains underexplored. DiffusionNFT optimizes instantaneous velocities, whereas MeanFlow samples with average velocities. To bridge this gap, we introduce MeanFlowNFT. Inspired by the MeanFlow identity, which bridges average and instantaneous velocities, we construct an induced instantaneous-velocity predictor. We apply the DiffusionNFT objective to this predictor, making reward optimization well-defined for MeanFlow. Sampling remains based on the average velocity, preserving MeanFlow's fast few-step generation. We further prove that MeanFlowNFT inherits DiffusionNFT's strict policy-improvement guarantee. Experiments on image and video generation show that MeanFlowNFT consistently improves baselines. Moreover, it outperforms prior state-of-the-art RL-tuned few-step generators on most metrics ($6$ of $8$ on SD3.5-M), and can even surpass multi-step RL-tuned diffusion while using only a few sampling steps. For instance, on Wan 2.1, $4$-step MeanFlowNFT reaches a VBench score of $84.33$, surpassing $50$-step LongCat-Video RL ($82.57$).

</details>


### [92] [Hierarchical Denoising For Multi-Step Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15278)
*Zezhong Qian,Xiaowei Chi,Chak-Wing Mak,Tianze Zhou,Ruibin Yuan,Yuhan Rui,Hengzhe Sun,Zhuoqun Wu,Yuming Li,Siyuan Qian,Sirui Han,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HDR（分层去噪视觉推理框架），通过树状层次化潜变量结构将粗到细的多步推理融入因果视频生成，在六个推理任务上成功率从34.22提升至60.29（相对提升76.2%），推理速度比双向扩散快54.2倍，仅用2%训练数据即可保留82.9%性能，并已在真实机器人实验中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频模型缺乏类似人类的多步推理能力：流式自回归扩散模型效率高但推理能力有限，双向扩散可全局修正但推理成本高。两者均难以在复杂推理任务中同时实现逻辑一致性和低延迟流式生成。

Method: 提出HDR框架：将视频潜变量组织为树状层次结构，粗去噪层保留不确定假设用于全局规划，细去噪层逐步精炼为具体视觉状态；设计稀疏层次注意力模式（SHAP）降低时序注意力成本；并构建包含6个任务的层次化多步视频推理基准。

Result: 流式自回归扩散基线成功率从34.22提升至60.29（相对增益76.2%），平均进度从76.00提升至89.56；推理延迟0.70秒/潜变量，比双向扩散快54.2倍；仅用2%训练数据保留82.9%性能（双向扩散为52.0%）；真实机器人实验验证物理交互与世界建模能力。

Conclusion: HDR证明了层次化潜变量结构能有效将多步推理能力注入因果视频生成模型，在保持低延迟流式输出的同时显著提升复杂推理的逻辑一致性，为视频基础模型向人类级推理演进提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Video models are evolving into vision foundation models, yet they still lack human-like multi-step reasoning. Streaming autoregressive diffusion models are efficient but limited in reasoning, while bidirectional diffusion enables global revision with high inference costs due to dense frame-level denoising. Both paradigms struggle to achieve logical consistency and low-latency streaming for complex reasoning tasks. We propose HDR (Hierarchical Denoising for Visual Reasoning), a unified framework that integrates hierarchical latents into causal video generation for multi-step reasoning. HDR organizes video latents into a tree-structured hierarchy, enabling coarse-to-fine reasoning before streaming output. Coarse denoising layers preserve uncertain hypotheses for global planning, while finer layers progressively refine them into concrete visual states. A sparse hierarchical attention pattern (SHAP) further reduces temporal attention costs. We introduce a level-stratified multi-step video reasoning benchmark with out-of-distribution cases, covering six tasks: maze navigation, Tower of Hanoi, one-line drawing, sliding puzzle, Sokoban, and water pouring. Compared with streaming autoregressive diffusion baselines, HDR improves success from 34.22 to 60.29 (76.2% relative gain) and increases average progress from 76.00 to 89.56, demonstrating more consistent reasoning trajectories. HDR maintains low-latency streaming at 0.70 seconds per latent, achieving 54.2 times faster inference than bidirectional diffusion. It also retains 82.9% of full-data performance with only 2% training data, compared with 52.0% for bidirectional diffusion. Real-world robot experiments further demonstrate HDR's potential for physical interaction and world modeling. Project demo: https://hierarchical-diffusion-reasoning.github.io/.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [93] [Just Keep Prompting: Evaluating Repetitive Socratic Prompting in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14099)
*Shayda Moezzi,Bishoy Galoaa,Lorena Genua,Taskin Padir,Sarah Ostadabbas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Just Keep Prompting (JKP)框架，通过多轮对抗性提问评估VLM的认知稳定性，发现重复提示会破坏模型推理稳定性，且不同模型表现出截然不同的压力响应模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM评估主要集中在单轮视觉推理能力，忽略了真实应用中用户反复质疑、追问等持续对话压力对模型稳定性的影响，缺乏系统性评估框架。

Method: 构建JKP多轮评估框架，包含三种提问策略：对抗性否定（反复拒绝）、纯苏格拉底式质询（反复要求重新评估）、上下文感知苏格拉底总结（回述先前推理后要求重新考虑）。在STAR基准子集上对GPT-4o、Gemini 2.5 Pro、Qwen3-VL-30B进行720次多轮运行评估。

Result: 整体准确率从第0轮到第10轮变化不大，但轨迹分析显示严重不稳定性：正确答案退化、错误答案恢复、多次答案翻转。重复提示的上限有限且往往起 destabilizer 作用。Qwen3-VL-30B最终准确率最高但在直接矛盾下自信犯错；Gemini 2.5 Pro相对稳定但token消耗大；GPT-4o最脆弱且振荡最严重。

Conclusion: 多轮VLM评估不仅能捕获额外推理能力，更能揭示模型的压力响应剖面：模型在视觉基础、校准和对话顺从性之间的权衡机制。

Abstract: Deploying Vision-Language Models (VLMs) in real-world settings requires not only strong visual reasoning but also stability under sustained conversational pressure. We introduce Just Keep Prompting (JKP), a multi-turn evaluation framework that measures VLM epistemic stability when users repeatedly challenge, question, or contradict a model's answer. JKP probes models for up to 10 follow-up turns using three strategies: Adversarial Negation (repeated rejection), Pure Socratic Interrogation (repeated calls to reassess certainty), and Context-Aware Socratic Summarization (reflecting the model's prior rationale back before asking for reconsideration). We evaluate GPT-4o, Gemini 2.5 Pro, and Qwen3-VL-30B on a subset of the STAR benchmark across 720 multi-turn runs. Aggregate accuracy changes modestly from Turn 0 to Turn 10, but trajectory-level analysis reveals substantial instability: correct answers regress, wrong answers recover, and many runs exhibit repeated answer flipping. Repeated prompting has bounded upside and often acts as a destabilizer rather than a reasoning aid. The effect is strongly model-dependent: Qwen3-VL-30B achieves the highest final accuracy but becomes confidently wrong under direct contradiction; Gemini 2.5 Pro is comparatively stable but token-expensive; GPT-4o is the most brittle and oscillatory. These findings reveal that multi-turn VLM evaluation captures not just additional reasoning but pressure-response profiles: how models trade off visual grounding, calibration, and conversational compliance under repeated challenge.

</details>


### [94] [Quantum Compositional NLP for Arabic: Grammar, Morphology, and Word Sense in Circuit Topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14100)
*Wajahath Mohammed*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次将基于预群语法的量子组合自然语言处理（QNLP）应用于阿拉伯语。阿拉伯语形态丰富、语序自由，为量子电路中的意义组合理论提供了高难度的测试环境。系统将阿拉伯语句子转换为量子电路，语法结构决定电路拓扑。通过三项控制实验（语序、形态时态、动词义消歧），与AraVec和AraBERT等经典基线对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将QNLP扩展到形态丰富、语序自由的阿拉伯语，验证其在该语言上的有效性，填补该领域空白。

Method: 使用预群语法将阿拉伯语句子映射为量子电路，语法类型依赖关系决定量子门连接方式；主语、动词、宾语对应量子门。

Result: 在语序、形态时态、动词义消歧三项实验中与AraVec和AraBERT基线进行了对比。

Conclusion: 该研究首次将QNLP应用于阿拉伯语，验证了预群语法量子方法在复杂语言结构上的可行性。

Abstract: We present the first application of pregroup grammar-based quantum compositional natural language processing (QNLP) to Arabic; a morphologically rich, free-word-order language whose structural complexity provides a uniquely demanding testbed for theories of meaning composition in quantum circuits. Our system converts Arabic sentences into quantum circuits whose topology mirrors grammatical structure: subjects, verbs, and objects become quantum gates, and the typed dependencies between them (the pregroup grammar) determine how those gates are wired together. We conduct three controlled experiments spanning word order, morphological tense, and verb sense disambiguation, comparing quantum circuit methods against classical baselines including AraVec (Arabic word embeddings) and AraBERT (a pre-trained Arabic transformer).

</details>


### [95] [LBA: Textual Hard-Label Adversarial Attack under Low Query Budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14101)
*Shixin Guo,Ming Zhong,Xuhong Zhang,Dandan Zhao,Zhe Wang,Bo Zhang,Shouling Ji,Hao Peng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种基于采样的低查询预算硬标签对抗文本生成方法LBA，通过整合先验和后验知识构建近似分布来采样高质量对抗样本，并在六种语言模型上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬标签场景下，现有贪婪局部搜索方法难以发现高质量对抗样本且查询成本过高，而穷举所有位置组合在计算上不可行，因此需要一种低查询预算下生成高质量对抗文本的有效方法。

Method: 提出LBA（Sampling-based Low-Budget Attack），通过整合先验知识和后验知识构建高质量对抗样本的近似分布，并利用该分布进行采样；采样过程中后验知识不断更新近似分布，引导更有效的采样。

Result: 在四个数据集上对六种从小到大规模的语言模型进行的实验表明，LBA在所有评估指标上显著优于最先进的基线方法；LLM评估显示LBA生成的对抗文本语义保留更好、可读性更高。

Conclusion: LBA通过基于采样的方法有效解决了硬标签场景下低查询预算的对抗文本生成问题，在攻击效果、查询效率和文本质量方面均取得显著提升。

Abstract: Generating high-quality adversarial texts with low query budgets remains a challenging problem in the hard-label scenario. Most existing approaches rely on greedy algorithms, where one position in the text is selected for substitution, followed by the substitutions of other positions. This local search approach may fail to discover high-quality adversarial examples and often leads to excessive query costs. Ideally, an optimal adversarial sample would consider all possible position combinations in the text, but exhaustive search is computationally impractical. To address this challenge, we propose a sampling-based method called LBA, which constructs an approximate distribution of high-quality adversarial examples by integrating both prior and posterior knowledge, and utilizes this distribution for sampling. As sampling progresses, posterior knowledge updates the approximate distribution, which in turn guides more effective sampling. Extensive experiments on six language models, ranging from small-scale to large-scale architectures across four datasets, demonstrate that LBA significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines on all evaluation metrics. Additionally, LLM-based assessment indicates that LBA generates more semantically preserved and comprehensible adversarial texts.

</details>


### [96] [UniSAGE: Unifying Static and Dynamic Attributes with Hyper-Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14102)
*Taoran Fang,Yan Deng,Chunping Wang,Yang Wang,Lei Chen,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: UniSAGE是一个统一框架，通过构建全局属性图、正交参数子空间和轻量级超结构机制，实现了对同时包含静态属性和动态属性的分层异构数据的自动建模，在多个基准和真实金融数据集上取得超10%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖大量人工设计、与特定数据模式紧密耦合，且通常将静态和动态属性分开处理，忽视了它们之间的隐式交互，无法以统一且可泛化的方式建模兼具静态属性和动态记录的分层异构数据。

Method: UniSAGE包含三个核心组件：1) 构建全局属性图，将层次关系和时序关系统一表示；2) 引入两个正交参数子空间，在共享语义空间中同时支持静态聚合和动态推理，确保表示一致性；3) 通过轻量级超结构机制实现静态与动态属性间的任务特定交互。

Result: 在多个公开基准和一个真实世界金融行为数据集上的实验表明，UniSAGE一致优于现有方法，在多个任务上实现了超过10%的性能提升。

Conclusion: UniSAGE完全自动化、对变化的数据模式具有鲁棒性，且能够捕获复杂的跨属性依赖关系，为静态与动态属性共存的异构数据建模提供了统一、高效且可泛化的解决方案。

Abstract: With the rapid growth of digital data, real-world applications increasingly involve hierarchical information that combines static attributes with dynamic records. Modeling such heterogeneous data in a unified and generalizable manner remains challenging. Existing approaches often rely on extensive manual design, are tightly coupled to specific data schemas, and typically process static and dynamic attributes in isolation, thereby overlooking their implicit interactions. We propose UniSAGE, a unified framework for modeling data with both static and dynamic attributes. UniSAGE constructs a global attribute graph that represents hierarchical and temporal relationships in a unified structure. To ensure representational consistency, it introduces two orthogonal parameter subspaces that jointly support static aggregation and dynamic reasoning within a shared semantic space. Building on these unified representations, UniSAGE further enables task-specific interaction between static and dynamic attributes via a lightweight hyper-structure mechanism. UniSAGE is fully automated, robust to evolving data schemas, and capable of capturing complex cross-attribute dependencies. Extensive experiments on multiple public benchmarks and a real-world financial behavior dataset demonstrate that UniSAGE consistently outperforms existing methods, achieving performance improvements of over 10% on several tasks.

</details>


### [97] [Latent Communication Between Language Model Agents: Channels, Alignment, and the Limits of Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14103)
*Markus Wenzel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征分析，量化了LLM智能体在文本通信中的信息损失，发现文本序列化会破坏88%的SAE特征，且丢失的特征主要编码表层形式而非任务相关语义，从而否定了“LLM拥有超越文本可表达性的世界模型”这一初始假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究假设大语言模型可能拥有超出文本可表达性的世界模型，特别是在需要传达复杂概念时。为了验证这一假设，作者希望通过结构化实验来证明文本通信存在信息损失，并探索潜在空间通信是否能保留更多信息。

Method: 构建三种通信通道（文本通道、密集潜在通道、SAE稀疏通道），使用SAE特征分析量化信息损失；通过稀疏潜在空间对齐进行跨架构通信实验；采用Procrustes对齐和非线性对齐方法；进行文本往返的特征存活分析；在跨语言概念任务上进行任务级评估。

Result: SAE稀疏通道在28倍压缩下保持99.4%的探针准确率，而文本通道仅为80.4%；Procrustes对齐下Llama和Mistral之间达到92%的top-1检索；文本序列化破坏88%的SAE特征；线性Procrustes对齐导致3-10百分点的性能惩罚；潜在通道在跨语言任务上与文本通道相当但未超越；文本增强潜在特征无获益。

Conclusion: 初始假设被否定：丢失的特征主要编码表层形式而非任务相关语义。潜在通信在现有任务上并未展现出超越文本通道的实用优势，需要对更深层次概念的任务和分析框架进行进一步研究。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) are utilized in many contexts and many professions. Those MAS rely on inter-agent communication, usually implemented by clear-text message passing. We hypothesize that Large Language Models may have a world model at their disposal that exceeds expressibility in text when complex concepts need to be communicated. Our aim is to approach a proof of this hypothesis with structured experiments. In this work, we show that LLM agents communicating via text lose information, which we quantify via Sparse Autoencoder (SAE) feature analysis. We construct three communication channels and measure concept-discriminating information in each. We first show that the SAE-sparse channel retains a 99.4% probe accuracy at 28-fold compression over the dense-latent channel vs 80.4% for the text channel. We then proceed to examine the same for cross-architecture communication by using sparse latent space alignment. We find for Procrustes alignment a 92% top-1 retrieval between Llama and Mistral. Using a text round-trip, we perform feature survival analysis to find that text serialization destroys 88% of SAE features, replacing them with a different feature set. We attribute the loss to identity replacement, not attenuation. By our analysis, we were able to attribute a 3-10pp performance penalty to the linear Procrustes alignment, improving with nonlinear alignment methods. In a task-level evaluation we find that the latent channel matches the text channel on cross-lingual concept tasks but never exceeds it. Text augmentation with latent features provides no benefit, leading us to negative conclusions for the initial hypothesis: lost features mostly or completely encode surface form, not task-relevant semantics. To pinpoint the practical advantage of latent communication over a text channel, deeper tasks eliciting complex concepts and an corresponding analysis framework are needed.

</details>


### [98] [UzWordnet and Generative AI for Learning Uzbek by Game Playing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14104)
*Alessandro Agostini,Saydobid Khusanov,Mirkamol Mirkamilov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过游戏化学习乌兹别克语的教育系统架构，该系统整合UzWordnet、正字词典和生成式AI，设计了四款教育游戏，并利用游戏机制反向改进和丰富词汇资源，同时实现语言学习和词汇资源优化双重目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前乌兹别克语学习资源有限，缺乏有效的数字化学习工具；同时UzWordnet等词汇资源也需要持续改进和丰富。作者试图通过游戏化学习的方式，同时解决语言教育和词汇资源建设两个问题。

Method: 提出一个教育系统架构，核心整合三大组件：UzWordnet（词汇网络）、最大的乌兹别克语正字词典以及生成式AI（学习支持）。基于此设计四款教育游戏，并设计了一套基于游戏动态的UzWordnet改进方法学。

Result: 设计了四款教育游戏用于乌兹别克语学习；游戏过程能直接产生UzWordnet的改进副产物；实现了教育与词汇资源建设的双目标协同。

Conclusion: 游戏设计与词汇资源相结合的方法可以同时服务于语言学习（教育目标）和词汇资源改进与丰富（词汇目标），验证了游戏机制在计算语言学和语言教育交叉领域的应用潜力。

Abstract: This paper presents an educational system architecture that enables learners to practice the Uzbek language through game-playing. The architecture integrates UzWordnet and the largest currently available orthographic dictionary for Uzbek as core lexical resources, together with generative AI as a fundamental component for learning support. We design four educational games to facilitate Uzbek language learning and propose a game-based methodology for improving UzWordnet as a direct by-product of game dynamics. Our approach combines game design and lexical resources to address objectives that are at the same time educational (language learning) and lexical (improvement and enrichment of a lexical resource).

</details>


### [99] [Automatically Evolving Prompt Guidelines for Task-Specific Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14105)
*Cedric Richter,Salah Ghamizi,Mike Papadakis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出AGOPS方法，通过自动生成任务特定的提示指南来缓解大语言模型中用户查询指定不足的问题。该方法利用已有参考答案中隐含的约束信息，通过优化方案进化提示指南，显著提升下游任务性能（平均提升15.5%-81.7%），而现有提示优化技术几乎无法恢复由指定不足导致的性能损失（高达95.3%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户查询常存在指定不足问题（underspecification），导致模型推断的隐含假设与用户实际意图不一致。现有提示工程指南大多是通用且任务无关的，且依赖人工手动制定，缺乏系统性和实用性。

Method: 提出AGOPS（Automatic Guideline Optimization via Prompt Specification），利用已有任务示例（参考答案）中隐含的缺失信息（行为约束、上下文假设、评估标准），通过包含提示编写LLM、求解器LLM和提示进化的优化方案，自动生成任务特定的提示指南。

Result: 在数学推理、医疗问答和代码任务上，指定不足导致下游性能下降高达95.3%，且现有提示优化技术几乎无法恢复。使用AGOPS指南后，用户可平均提升15.5%-81.7%的性能，在所有基准测试上表现一致。

Conclusion: AGOPS能有效自动生成任务特定指南，帮助用户编写更明确的提示，显著恢复因指定不足导致的性能损失，具有跨任务的一致有效性。

Abstract: For Large Language Models to reliably answer user queries, users must clearly specify requirements, context, and constraints. In practice, however, user queries are often underspecified, forcing models to infer unstated assumptions that may misalign with the actual user intent. Existing prompt engineering guidelines aim to mitigate this issue, they are typically generic and task-agnostic, limiting their practical utility. Additionally, existing guidelines are formed manually and in a non-systematic way. To this end, we study prompt guideline optimization: the problem of automatically generating task-specific guidelines that help write better-specified prompts for a given task and model. Our key observation is that existing (completed) task examples (aka reference answers) often implicitly encode the missing information required to complete underspecified queries, including behavioral constraints, contextual assumptions, and evaluation criteria. We therefore propose AGOPS, an automatic approach that evolves task-specific guidelines via an optimization scheme that involves a prompt LLM writer, a solver LLM and prompt evolution, which maximize downstream effectiveness on a set of examples (user queries with reference answers). At inference time, our guidelines help users write well-specified prompts, boosting the effectiveness of LLMs. We show across mathematical reasoning, medical question answering, and coding tasks, that prompt underspecification leads to major drops (up to 95.3%) in downstream task performance (compared to well-specified prompts) and, perhaps more importantly, that this drop can hardly be recovered by existing prompt optimization techniques. Users following AGOPS guidelines can regain this loss (increasing performance between 15.5 to 81.7% on average) consistently across all benchmarks.

</details>


### [100] [Token Time Continuous Diffusion for Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14106)
*Parikshit Bansal,Sujay Sanghavi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出token时间连续扩散（TTCD），一种在连续空间中操作并引入逐token时间概念的新型扩散语言模型，在高加速条件下显著优于离散模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散扩散语言模型在高加速条件下因并行采样多个token导致精度下降，且缺乏对条件生成和token间差异化影响的精细建模能力。

Method: TTCD在连续空间中将高斯噪声确定性映射到最终token画布，无需额外采样；引入逐token时间机制，允许不同token以不同速率从噪声演变为最终token，从而更好地建模条件生成和token间差异化影响。

Result: 160M参数的TTCD模型在OpenWebText上训练并自蒸馏后，在高加速条件下无条件和条件生成质量与多个同类模型相当或更优，在数独求解任务上也取得相似增益。

Conclusion: 连续空间建模与逐token时间机制的结合有效提升了扩散语言模型在高加速场景下的生成质量，尤其在条件生成任务中优势显著。

Abstract: In this paper we introduce token time continuous diffusion (TTCD), a new diffusion language model which (a) operates in continuous space, deterministically mapping Gaussian noise to a final token canvas with no further sampling, and crucially (b) incorporates a new notion of per-token times, with some tokens proceeding from noise to token at a faster rate than others. Continuous space modeling helps TTCD avoid the parallel sampling of multiple tokens, which is a key source of inaccuracy at high speedups for models that iterate purely in discrete space. The notion of per-token times helps TTCD to better model conditional generation, allows for more sure tokens to proceed at a faster rate, and allows for differentiated inter-token influences during refinement. TTCD outperforms discrete models at high speedups. We train a 160M parameter TTCD model on OpenWebText, and then self-distill it; we find that at high speedups we are comparable in unconditional generation quality, and outperform in conditional generation, several existing models of similar size trained, on the same data, and self-distilled. We achieve similar gains in Sudoku solving as well.

</details>


### [101] [Polestar: Drift-Aware Cache Calibration and Token Commitment for Efficient Inference of Diffusion LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14107)
*Mingyu Lee,Akshat Ramachandran,Souvik Kundu,Tushar Krishna*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对扩散大语言模型推理效率的两个挑战（KV缓存复用效率低、静态阈值并行解码损害质量），提出基于token表示漂移的统一信号框架Polestar，包含Polestar-Cache（稀疏KV缓存刷新）和Polestar-Commit（检测漂移事件确定就绪token），在不需额外训练的情况下实现精度-吞吐量帕累托前沿最优，最高提升10.73%精度、3.7倍吞吐量及每前向3.67个token的并行解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型的双向注意力导致KV缓存难以高效复用，且使用静态置信度阈值增加解码并行度会损害生成质量，这两个挑战均源于token表示在解码步骤间的漂移现象。

Method: 提出Polestar框架，利用token表示漂移作为统一信号：1) Polestar-Cache通过漂移检测识别陈旧KV缓存位置并进行稀疏刷新；2) Polestar-Commit通过检测剧烈漂移事件可靠识别可提交token。

Result: 在数学和编程基准测试上，多个dLLM家族中达到精度-吞吐量帕累托前沿最优，精度提升最高10.73%，吞吐量提升最高3.7倍，解码并行度达每前向3.67个token。

Conclusion: Polestar通过统一建模token表示漂移，有效同时解决了dLLM推理中的KV缓存复用和并行解码质量两大瓶颈，无需训练即可显著提升效率。

Abstract: The inference efficiency of diffusion large language models (dLLMs) is constrained by two challenges: bidirectional attention precludes efficient KV-cache reuse, while increasing decoding parallelism with static confidence thresholds can compromise generation quality. We observe that both challenges arise from a shared phenomenon: as tokens are decoded, their contextual integration through bidirectional attention causes token representations to drift (evolve) across decoding steps. This insight motivates Polestar, a training-free inference framework that uses token representation drift as a unified signal to jointly address both challenges. Polestar comprises two components: Polestar-Cache, which identifies stale KV-cache positions via drift and performs sparse KV-cache refreshes to enable efficient reuse, and Polestar-Commit, which detects sharp drift events to reliably identify commit-ready tokens. Across mathematics and coding benchmarks on several dLLM families, Polestar sets a new state of the art on the accuracy-throughput Pareto frontier, achieving up to 10.73% accuracy improvement, up to 3.7x higher throughput, and high decoding parallelism of 3.67 tokens per forward pass over existing baselines.

</details>


### [102] [Eta Given Delta: Defining LLM Tool Efficiency With Marginal Tool Utility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14108)
*Nyx Iskandar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了工具效率（tool efficiency）这一新的量化指标，用于评估LLM智能体轨迹中有用工具调用的比率。同时引入边际工具效用（marginal tool utility）指标，通过LLM-as-a-Judge判断每次工具调用是否有用，从而直接量化测量效率，而非像以往工作那样以准确率作为间接代理指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作主要集中在开发提升LLM工具使用的技术，并通过准确率等间接代理指标来评估效率，缺乏直接测量工具调用效率的量化指标。

Method: 提出工具效率和边际工具效用两个量化指标，利用LLM-as-a-Judge判断每次工具调用的边际效用正负，从而在事后轨迹分析中直接评估工具使用效率。

Result: 提出了可直接量化评估工具调用效率的指标体系，为后续基准测试设计和智能体工具集优化提供了新的度量标准。

Conclusion: 该工作旨在推动LLM评估研究的前沿，为未来基准设计及智能体工程（特别是精简工具集构建）提供与准确率互补但不同的优化指标。

Abstract: This paper introduces tool efficiency, a new quantitative metric to evaluate the rate of useful tool calls in an LLM agent trajectory. To ensure that tool efficiency is well-defined, we also introduce marginal tool utility, a new quantitative metric defined per tool call indicating whether a tool is useful or whether it can be safely removed from the tool suite without affecting accuracy while increasing tool efficiency; in this paper, we determine the sign of marginal tool utility for each tool call in a trajectory using LLM-as-a-Judge. While much prior work has been done to develop techniques that improve tool use by LLMs and design evaluation methods measuring efficiency indirectly using accuracy as a proxy, our work is centered on measuring efficiency directly via the quantitative metric proposed in this paper in post hoc trajectory analyses. It is our intention that this work contributes to the frontier of LLM evaluation research as a springboard for future benchmark designs and agent harness engineering (specifically with regards to creating lean tool suites) that optimize for metrics that complement but are distinct from accuracy.

</details>


### [103] [Simplicity Paradox: Debunking myths about prompting and datasets for LLM evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14109)
*Inder Preet,Shuxin Lin,Dhaval Patel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对8种提示技术在10个MCQA数据集上进行了大规模实证研究（27个模型配置、43万次评估），发现基线提示始终优于复杂推理方法，只有CoT-Expert和CoT-Inductive带来约3个百分点的微弱提升，而其他复杂技术甚至低于基线（Self-Analogical最多低31个百分点）。研究还揭示了Qwen3-30B-A3B-Thinking-2507在Elo评分中的意外胜出、性能-效率权衡以及数据集难度差异（60%低于70%准确率）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究默认更复杂的提示技术性能更好，但缺乏全面评估，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在10个MCQA数据集上对8种提示技术进行系统对比，涉及27个模型配置和约4300个独特问题，总计超过43万次评估。

Result: 基线提示优于大多数复杂推理技术；仅CoT-Expert和CoT-Inductive带来约3个百分点的显著提升；Self-Analogical最多低31个百分点；Qwen3-30B-A3B-Thinking-2507意外获得最高Elo评分；60%数据集准确率低于70%。

Conclusion: 提示工程可能被过度复杂化，真正的提升空间在于模型本身改进而非提示优化。

Abstract: Probing the capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs) and building robust solutions for Multiple-Choice Question Answering (MCQA) remain central challenges in natural language understanding. Furthermore, the rapid proliferation of LLMs has created the implicit assumption that more sophisticated prompting techniques yield better performance. Several studies claim better performance with more sophisticated prompting techniques, but do not provide a comprehensive evaluation. We address this gap through a comprehensive empirical study of 8 prompting techniques across 10 multiple-choice question answering (MCQA) datasets, encompassing 27 model configurations and roughly 4,300 unique questions evaluated more than 430,000 times. Our findings reveal a striking paradox that baseline prompting consistently outperforms complex reasoning techniques on various benchmarks. Only minimal expert and inductive role framing (CoT-Expert and CoT-Inductive) yields a small but statistically significant $\sim$3 percentage-point (pp) gain over baseline whereas every other elaborate technique we tested matches or under-performs it, often by large margins (up to 31~pp for Self-Analogical). We further investigate three critical phenomena: (1) the unexpected victory of Qwen3-30B-A3B-Thinking-2507 in Elo ratings, (2) the performance-efficiency trade-offs across model variants with different thinking budgets, revealing model-dependent optimal configurations, and (3) the substantial variation in dataset difficulty, with 60% of benchmarks below 70% accuracy and a 47.5~pp spread from easiest to hardest, indicating considerable room for model improvement. These results suggest that the LLM evaluation community may be overcomplicating prompt engineering and that substantial performance gaps remain across diverse benchmarks, offering opportunities for genuine model improvements rather than prompt optimization.

</details>


### [104] [MAPS: Modeling Co-Existing Subjective Perspectives and Shared Meaning in Multi-Agent Cognitive Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14110)
*Molood Arman,Clément Bonnafous*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MAPS是一个多代理视角空间框架，通过加权领域画像、GRU动态记忆和可解释的token级注意力机制，使认知各异的代理在对话中保持个性化推理的同时渐进收敛于共享语义，在三个数据集上实现了语义对齐与主观表达间的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI对话系统强制语义统一，牺牲了表达的多样性与可解释性，而人类对话天然包含信念、情感和主观认知风格的交流。

Method: 提出MAPS框架，包含三个核心组件：领域加权画像（domain-weighted profiles）建模代理的认知特征，GRU动态记忆（dynamic GRU-based memory）追踪对话历史，以及可解释的token级注意力（interpretable token-level attention）实现语义渐进收敛。

Result: 在EmpatheticDialogues、TopicalChat和MultiWOZ三个基准数据集上验证了MAPS能够实现语义对齐而不坍塌主体性。

Conclusion: MAPS展示了构建认知基础、可解释且兼具表达力与连贯性的对话系统的可行路径。

Abstract: Human dialogue involves more than exchanging information; it also expresses beliefs, emotions, and subjective cognitive styles. Yet current AI dialogue systems often enforce semantic uniformity, sacrificing diversity and interpretability. We present MAPS (Multi-Agent Perspective Spaces), a novel framework that models dialogue between cognitively distinct agents through domain-weighted profiles, dynamic GRU-based memory, and interpretable token-level attention. MAPS enables agents to maintain individualized reasoning while progressively converging on shared meaning. Evaluations on EmpatheticDialogues, TopicalChat, and MultiWOZ show that MAPS supports semantic alignment without collapsing subjectivity. Our results demonstrate a path toward cognitively grounded, interpretable dialogue systems that balance expressiveness and coherence.

</details>


### [105] [Introspection Fine-Tuning (IFT): Training Small LLMs to Introspect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14111)
*Ely Hahami,Ishaan Sinha,Lavik Jain*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究小语言模型能否检测和报告自身内部激活中的扰动。通过激活引导注入概念向量，作者发现先前二分类检测范式存在混杂问题（引导偏向肯定回答），于是提出句子定位和强度比较两种无混杂评估范式。实验表明2B以上模型具备高于随机水平的自省能力。作者提出内省微调（IFT），通过模型自身扰动前向传播构建监督数据微调，使Llama-1B定位准确率从9.6%提升至60.6%，且零样本泛化到新任务，同时标准能力benchmark几乎无损。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究小语言模型能否检测和报告自身内部激活中的扰动，以及这种自省能力是否可以通过训练而非仅依赖模型规模来提升。

Method: 提出两种无混杂评估范式：句子定位（从N个句子中识别被扰动句）和强度比较（判断两个句子中哪个受到更强注入）。提出内省微调（IFT），利用模型自身扰动前向传播构建句子定位的监督数据微调模型。

Result: Llama-1B经IFT后句子定位准确率从9.6%提升至60.6%（6倍提升），零样本泛化到强度比较任务（30.2%→52.2%）。IFT对3B和8B模型也有提升，且标准能力benchmark退化可忽略。

Conclusion: 自省能力并非仅由模型规模决定，可以通过直接训练来提升，这为解锁潜在自我监控能力提供了可能，对AI透明度和对齐有重要意义。

Abstract: Can small language models detect and report on perturbations their own internal activations? We investigate this question through the lens of activation steering: injecting concept vectors into a model's residual stream and measuring whether the model can accurately report on the perturbation. We first show that the binary detection paradigm used in prior work -- prompting the model to answer Yes'' or No'' to whether it detects an injected thought -- is confounded in small models, as steering biases the model toward affirmative responses regardless of the question content. We therefore propose two confound-free evaluation paradigms: sentence localization (identifying which of $N$ sentences was perturbed, chance $= 1/N$) and strength comparison (identifying which of two sentences received a stronger injection, chance $= 50\%$). Evaluating across six models from two families (Llama-3.2 and Gemma-4), we find that models as small as 2B parameters introspect reliably well above chance, and that introspective ability generally increases with scale. Llama-1B, however, performs at or below chance. We then introduce \emph{Introspection Fine-Tuning} (IFT): supervised fine-tuning on sentence-localization examples constructed from the model's own perturbed forward passes. IFT raises Llama-1B sentence-localization accuracy from $9.6\%$ to $60.6\%$ (a $6\times$ improvement), with gains generalizing zero-shot to the held-out strength-comparison task ($30.2\% \to 52.2\%$). IFT also improves introspection for 3B and 8B models, while inducing negligible degradation on standard capability benchmarks. Our results suggest that introspective ability is not fixed by scale alone: it can be directly trained, and doing so unlocks latent self-monitoring capacity with implications for AI transparency and alignment. Our code is \href{https://anonymous.4open.science/r/IFT-introspection-2092/README.md}{here}.

</details>


### [106] [Information-Theoretic Limits of Reliability and Scaling in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14112)
*Subhabrata Majumdar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文从信息论角度证明，生成式语言模型存在不可超越的可靠性上限，该上限由上下文可解析的不确定性和任务固有歧义共同决定，并推导出自回归生成退化率与依赖核的关系，最终建立以数据或容量为瓶颈的首要原理缩放定律，统一解释多种实践现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估假设LLM在足够规模下可达到完美可靠性，本文从信息论出发证明这一假设不成立，旨在刻画生成任务固有的可靠性上限及其影响因素。

Method: 将输出不确定性分解为可解析分量（可通过增加上下文消除）和主观分量（任务固有歧义）；引入依赖核（dependency kernel）量化自回归生成中令牌间相关性导致的性能退化；基于上述两个原始概念推导首要原理缩放定律。

Result: 推导出以训练数据或模型容量为瓶颈的缩放定律，Chinchilla定律为其特例；该框架统一解释了检索增强、灾难性遗忘的谱机制等现象。

Conclusion: 本文形式化了生成式语言模型性能的资源-复杂度权衡理论，提供了一个统一的理论框架来理解模型在各类任务中的性能极限。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are evaluated as though perfect reliability is achievable for any task given sufficient scale. We show this assumption is information-theoretically unjustified. Every generative task has a reliability ceiling that no model can exceed, determined by how much output uncertainty is resolvable from observable context. The gap decomposes into a resolvable component closable with additional context and a subjective component inherent to task ambiguity. Autoregressive generation further degrades this ceiling at a rate governed by the task's dependency kernel, which quantifies inter-token correlations in the output. From these two primitives, we derive a first-principles scaling law where LLM performance is bottlenecked by the scarcer resource: training data or model capacity. This law recovers the Chinchilla scaling law as a special case and provides a structural account of when scaling improves reliability. Beyond scaling, our framework unifies diverse practical phenomena, such as the benefits of retrieval-augmentation and the spectral mechanics of catastrophic forgetting. Our work formalizes the resource-complexity tradeoffs that govern model performance across domains, offering a unified theory of performance limits in generative language models.

</details>


### [107] [T5-CSBoost: Adversarial Perturbation Resistant LLM Fingerprinting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14113)
*Gayan K. Kulatilleke,Mahsa Baktashmotlagh,Siamak Layeghy,Marius Portmann*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: T5-CSBoost 通过对比学习正则化解码器嵌入，在不修改 T5-small 骨干网络的情况下，显著提升了 AIGT 检测在多分类溯源、二分类检测以及对抗扰动（单词/字符编辑、极端改写）下的鲁棒性，在多个基准上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AIGT 检测器在干净输入上表现良好，但在轻度改写、词汇替换、字符编辑和分布偏移下准确率大幅下降，亟需一种轻量且有效的鲁棒检测方法。

Method: 基于 T5-Sentinel 框架，保留原始下一词元预测目标用于源归因，同时在解码器嵌入上引入辅助的基于间距的三元组损失，通过对比风格正则化学习紧凑且抗扰动的风格化表征。

Result: 在 OpenLLMText 和 HC3 基准上取得多分类源归因和二元人机检测 SOTA；在 MAGE/Deepfake 压力测试中（包含未见模型、未见领域、极端改写）对高达 90% 强度的词级和字符级对抗扰动表现出增强的鲁棒性，达到 SOTA。

Conclusion: 通过对比学习显式正则化风格嵌入是构建更鲁棒的 LLM 指纹识别系统的实用且有效策略。

Abstract: While many AI-generated text (AIGT) detectors achieve strong performance on clean inputs, their accuracy degrades significantly under light paraphrasing, word substitutions, character edits, and distribution shifts. We present T5 Contrastive Style Boosted Classifier (T5-CSBoost), an extension to the T5-Sentinel framework that keeps the original next-token prediction objective for source attribution while introducing an auxiliary margin-based triplet loss over decoder embeddings.
  This contrastive style regularization encourages the learning of compact, perturbation-resistant stylistic representations, offering a lightweight yet effective alternative to prior approaches that rely on architectural modifications, adversarial training, or complex multi-task objectives without altering the underlying T5-small backbone. T5-CSBoost achieves state-of-the-art multiclass source attribution and binary human-vs-LLM detection on OpenLLMText and HC3 AIGT benchmarks. More importantly, T5-CSBoost demonstrates enhanced robustness to word and character level adversarial perturbations of up to 90% intensity, achieving state-of-the-art on the challenging MAGE/Deepfake stress-test suite, including unseen models, unseen domains, and extreme paraphrasing scenarios. Our results highlight that explicitly regularizing stylistic embeddings via contrastive learning is a practical and effective strategy for building more robust LLM fingerprinting systems in real-world adversarial settings.

</details>


### [108] [CoEvoT: Co-Evolving Chain-of-Thought Prompting for Graph-LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14114)
*Haohua Niu,Xingtong Yu,Yang Liu,Junfeng Fang,Xuanting Xie,Jie Tan,Zhongjian Zhang,Hong Cheng,Yuan Fang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CoEvoT提出了一种协同演化的思维链提示框架，通过文本到图令牌重写和图到文本推理指导的闭环机制，实现了逐步、状态感知的证据细化，在8个数据集上持续超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于思维链的图-大语言模型方法在生成中间推理步骤时依赖固定的图令牌，无法实现结构线索的逐步优化，限制了模型在分布偏移下对图结构的适应能力。

Method: CoEvoT构建文本到图令牌重写与图到文本推理指导的闭环：每个中间文本思维通过轻量条件网络更新图令牌证据状态，更新后的令牌反馈至下一步指令以指导后续LLM推理，实现逐步、状态感知的证据精炼。

Result: 在8个数据集上的实验表明，CoEvoT持续优于最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: CoEvoT通过协同演化的思维链机制有效解决了固定图结构推理的局限性，显著提升了图-大语言模型在分布偏移下的标签高效学习能力。

Abstract: Graph learning under distribution shift presents a persistent challenge, where models adapt to new graphs with limited or even no supervision.
